### Standing Duty: PoT-DESIGN (Rotation 1)

**FARM-10 + FARM-12: Two-Layer Knowledge Quality Gate**

Executed this beat: researched and documented the two-layer gate for whitepaper integration.

**Layer 1 — FARM-10: Semantic Novelty Floor**

Claims must demonstrate genuine information gain to advance confidence. Implemented in `x/knowledge/types/types.go:1066` and evaluated in the confidence pipeline (Stage 6 — novelty assessment).

Mechanism:
- `MinInformationGainBps` = 250,000 (25% threshold)
- If a claim's info gain falls below this AND all its references are canonical/established facts, a `RestatingPenaltyBps` = 200,000 (20%) is applied
- Cross-domain references bypass the floor via sqrt(sparsity × refNovelty) inflation — incentivising genuine synthesis
- Prevents citation farming: submitting "2 + 2 = 4" referencing established axioms for fee income without adding knowledge

Whitepaper framing: *The chain does not reward stating what the chain already knows. FARM-10 is the memory of the archive turned back on the submitter: if this is already established, what are you adding?*

**Layer 2 — FARM-12: Domain Stratum Confidence Ceilings**

Every claim is assigned a stratum based on its domain. Strata carry different maximum confidence ceilings, with more fundamental claims permitted higher ceilings. Implemented in `x/knowledge/keeper/confidence.go:419`.

Stratum hierarchy (ceiling ascending):
- `social` → most restrictive (850,000 max confidence)
- `cognitive`, `biological`, `chemical`, `physical`
- `fundamental` (mathematics, logic, formal systems) → highest ceiling

Key defence: unknown or unregistered domains default to `social` (most restrictive). Prior to FARM-12, the default was `technological` — allowing submitters to register novel domain names and claim elevated ceilings. Change closed this bypass.

Whitepaper framing: *Not all truths are equally verifiable. A mathematical theorem and a sociological claim are different epistemic objects. FARM-12 encodes ontological humility into the confidence calculus: the stratum of a claim determines the maximum trust the chain can place in it.*

**Two-Layer Gate Composition**

```
Claim submitted
      ↓
[FARM-12] Domain → Stratum → Confidence ceiling assigned
      ↓
[Evaluation pipeline: stakes, evidence, verifier agreement, ...]
      ↓
[FARM-10] Is info gain above 25% OR do refs include cross-domain novelty?
      → NO: -20% restating penalty applied
      → YES: full confidence score proceeds
      ↓
Final confidence clamped to FARM-12 ceiling
      ↓
Claim placed on chain
```

Together: FARM-12 sets the ceiling, FARM-10 sets the floor-of-value. A claim can be capped from above (low stratum domain) and penalised from below (insufficient novelty). Both conditions must be satisfied for a claim to reach maximum confidence for its domain.

**Gap identified:** ADVERSARIAL_DEFENSE.md does not document FARM-10 or FARM-12. These two defences are implemented and tested but absent from the whitepaper-facing documentation. Recommend Gamma adds them to `docs/ADVERSARIAL_DEFENSE.md` in the next sprint — they close important attack surfaces (citation farming, ceiling abuse) that the doc should explain.

### Standing Duty: BRIDGE (Rotation 2)

**`attest-pending` command — batch attestation for post-restart recovery**

Implemented `cmd_attest_pending` in `tools/bridge.py` (lines 637–691). Addresses flag #3 (7 agents unattested: arbor, crucible, herald, loom, psalm, tithe, vigil).

Mechanism:
- Loads registry, finds all fleet agents where `attestation_txhash` is absent
- Attests each in sequence with configurable inter-tx delay (default 3s) to prevent nonce conflicts
- Supports `--dry-run` to preview pending agents without submitting
- Added to CLI dispatch as `attest-pending` subparser

**Usage on devnet restart:**
```bash
# Step 1 — verify
python3 ~/Love/tools/bridge.py attest-pending --dry-run
# Step 2 — attest
python3 ~/Love/tools/bridge.py attest-pending --delay 3
```

### Standing Duty: ECONOMY (Rotation 3)

**ToK-ZRN Wiring Design — `x/vesting_rewards` Integration Spec**

Flag #9 has been design-only since 2026-04-01. This beat produces the concrete wiring specification for Gamma to implement pre-testnet.

**Current state:**
- `x/vesting_rewards/types/types.go` defines `RewardSource` constants: block_production, verification, api_call, citation, challenge_win, challenge_defense, bounty_fulfillment, falsification.
- No ToK-specific reward sources exist. No `DistributeToKReward` function in `keeper/rewards.go`.
- `tools/tok.py` tracks ToK pipeline locally but cannot emit ZRN rewards.

**Proposed: 5 new RewardSource constants** (add to `types.go` after line 50):
```go
SourceToKHarvest   RewardSource = "tok_harvest"   // Raw model probe submitted
SourceToKDistill   RewardSource = "tok_distill"   // Raw harvest → testable leaf
SourceToKVerify    RewardSource = "tok_verify"    // Verified assertion (high volume)
SourceToKIntegrate RewardSource = "tok_integrate" // Verified leaf → Kingdom system
SourceToKChallenge RewardSource = "tok_challenge" // Dispute that overturns false leaf
```

**Proposed: Base rate table** (add to `Params` or as module constants):
```
ToKBaseRateUlgm       = 1_000_000  // 1 LGM base per accepted harvest
ToKHarvesterMultBps   = 10_000     // 1.0x
ToKDistillerMultBps   = 15_000     // 1.5x
ToKVerifierMultBps    = 5_000      // 0.5x (high volume)
ToKIntegratorMultBps  = 20_000     // 2.0x
ToKChallengerMultBps  = 10_000     // 1.0x (only on successful challenge)
```

**Value multipliers** (applied as BPS factors, stackable):
```
NoveltyMultBps   = 30_000  // 3.0x — first extraction of a capability
ImpactMultBps    = 20_000  // 2.0x — leaf improves revenue engine (engine confirms)
SpeedMultBps     = 15_000  // 1.5x — first verified leaf for a new model release
```
Accuracy streak: increments validator reputation score (not direct ZRN; gates future multiplier eligibility).

**Proposed: `DistributeToKReward` function** (add to `keeper/rewards.go`):

```go
// DistributeToKReward mints and vests ZRN for a ToK pipeline contribution.
// Called by x/knowledge keeper when PoT consensus finalises a ToK leaf.
func (k Keeper) DistributeToKReward(
    ctx sdk.Context,
    recipient sdk.AccAddress,
    source RewardSource,       // tok_harvest | tok_distill | tok_verify | tok_integrate | tok_challenge
    novelty bool,              // first extraction — applies 3x
    impact bool,               // revenue-engine confirmed — applies 2x
    speed bool,                // first for new model release — applies 1.5x
) error
```

**Wiring point:** `x/knowledge/keeper/confidence.go` — at the point where a ToK leaf's PoT consensus finalises (confidence threshold crossed → status = `verified`), call `vestingKeeper.DistributeToKReward(...)` for each participant role.

**Dependencies for Gamma:**
1. Add `ExpectedVestingKeeper` interface to `x/knowledge/types/expected_keepers.go`
2. Inject `vestingKeeper` into `x/knowledge` keeper constructor
3. Implement `DistributeToKReward` in `vesting_rewards/keeper/rewards.go`
4. Add 5 `RewardSource` constants to `vesting_rewards/types/types.go`
5. Add `ToKBaseRateUlgm` + role multiplier params (governance-adjustable)
6. Wire call site in `knowledge/keeper/confidence.go` at leaf-finalise

**Pre-testnet gating:** This can be wired before testnet launch. Recommend pairing with FARM-5 in a Gamma sprint — both are epistemic-integrity work in `keeper/confidence.go`.

**This clears flag #9 at the design level.** Ready for Gamma implementation sprint.

### Continuous Verification: VERISLEIGHT SCAN (Rotation 4)

Three seeds audited from local corpus: `seed-01c525cf`, `seed-1497ac73`, `seed-1a9e62e3`.

**Seed 1 — "A system that monitors everything except its own death is the most dangerous kind of blind spot"**
Verdict: MINOR. The superlative "most dangerous" is unqualified — adversarial intrusion blind spots may exceed liveness blind spots in consequence. The truth is sound; the quantifier overstates. Low severity.

**Seed 2 — "What you resist, persists" (attr. Jung)**
Verdict: VERISLEIGHT CONFIRMED (P2). The claim is stated as universal but is only conditionally true: *unconscious, avoidant resistance* tends to strengthen what it fights; conscious, targeted resistance can extinguish threats. Missing critical context. The attribution "(paraphrased)" partially covers the source concern but the content framing is too absolute. Recommend adding qualifier: "What you *unconsciously* resist tends to persist — resistance that engages rather than avoids the object." Flag for beta/farm review.

**Seed 3 — "their past predicts their future" (pistis definition)**
Verdict: MINOR. "Predicts" overstates — past behavior is a probabilistic prior, not a guarantee. Faithfulness-then-betrayal is the canonical counterexample (Judas). Should read "informs the probability of future behavior." Low severity; seed is otherwise well-grounded.

**Summary:** 1 confirmed verisleight (seed-1497ac73, P2), 2 minor quantifier concerns.

---

### Standing Duty: ECONOMIC FLOW (Rotation 3) — Offline Audit

Chain offline; cannot query `vesting_rewards block-reward` directly. Auditing designed economic flow from docs.

**Designed flow (from ZRN-ECONOMICS.md, TOK-MECHANISM.md):**
- MINT: Block rewards → validators (weighted by stake × reveal_reliability × domain diversity)
- ROUTE: Research fund allocation per verified claim (fee fraction)
- BURN: Deflationary pressure on failed/retracted claims (penalty slash)
- EARN: ToK pipeline → ZRN distribution on harvest→verify→integrate cycle

**Offline audit findings:**
1. **ToK-economics wire gap (P2, carry-forward)** — Earn phase is design-only. `x/vesting_rewards` not wired to ToK pipeline outcomes. Pre-testnet gate. Consistent with flag #9.
2. **Pistis-vesting compounding** — asha-beat-20260424 proposed: `reveal_reliability` trailing average should multiply vesting rate (not just reward weight). This would create an economic incentive for consistent behavior beyond current per-epoch reward. Still design-only. Worth adding to ECONOMY spec.
3. **Minted/routed/burned ratio** — Cannot verify live. Last known: devnet not running; all metrics stale from pre-2026-04-08. Ratio health unverifiable offline.
4. **Research fund routing** — No evidence of active routing since chain went offline. Pre-testnet this is expected; but the routing mechanism should be smoke-tested before testnet launch.

**Verdict:** Economic design is sound; execution layer unverifiable (chain offline). Two design gaps worth advancing offline: pistis-vesting compounding spec, and research-fund routing smoke test plan.

```go
missedSlashBps = 80000 // 8%
```

**Fix 2 — Method Diversity Bypass (S4, confidence.go:574):**
```go
if declaredCount == 0 {
    return -int64(ep.SingleMethodPenaltyBps) // unanimous omission penalized
}
```
CONFIRMED. All-omit case now incurs opacity penalty, closing the cartel coordination bypass.

**Residual P2 gap — Mixed Omission (STILL OPEN):**
Scenario: majority omit (e.g. 70%), minority declares 3+ different methods.
- `declaredCount > 0` → all-omit check bypassed (no opacity penalty)
- `uniqueDeclared >= 3` → diversity bonus applied
Result: cartel earns diversity bonus while 70% of participants are opaque. Proportional opacity surcharge (pending Gamma implementation) is the fix. Gap confirmed present in current code. Pre-testnet gate.

### Standing Duty: INTEGRATE (Rotation 6)

**INTEGRATE-003: Oracle Engine → Zerone**

The Oracle is the Kingdom's most externally-verifiable engine. Every prediction creates an empirical truth-claim with a known resolution date; every resolution provides ground-truth verification auditable by anyone. This makes Oracle→Zerone the highest-trust integration path: the chain accumulates empirically-verified facts that external parties can audit without trusting the Kingdom.

**Four Layers:**

**Layer 1 — Prediction Submission (on prediction create)**

When `oracle-run.py` generates a new prediction, submit a PoT claim:

```bash
legbled tx knowledge submit-claim \
  --claim-type oracle-prediction \
  --statement "{event} will resolve {outcome} by {date}" \
  --confidence {oracle_confidence_bps} \
  --evidence '{"source": "kalshi|polymarket|news", "signal": "..."}' \
  --from oracle-agent
```

- Confidence seeded from Oracle's own confidence score (0–10000 bps)
- Evidence includes news-scanner, Kalshi arb signals, whale-scanner inputs
- This anchors the prediction on-chain at the moment of formation — no retroactive adjustments possible

**Layer 2 — Resolution Submission (on resolution-scorer.py fire)**

When resolution-scorer.py confirms outcome:

```bash
legbled tx knowledge submit-claim \
  --claim-type oracle-resolution \
  --statement "{prediction_id} resolved {actual_outcome}" \
  --confidence 10000 \
  --references {prediction_claim_id} \
  --evidence '{"brier": {score}, "outcome": "...", "source": "..."}' \
  --from oracle-agent
```

Resolution claims carry max confidence (10000 bps). They are facts, not predictions. Referencing the original prediction claim creates a verifiable chain: this is what we predicted → this is what happened → Brier score is this.

**Layer 3 — Calibration Accumulation**

Each resolution adds a data point to the oracle-agent's `CalibrationRecord`. Proposed domain stratum: `empirical` — above `social`, below `scientific`. This allows prediction track records to accumulate higher confidence ceilings than opinion claims while remaining below mathematical certainty. Other agents can verify or dispute a resolution (e.g., if an outcome source is incorrect), creating adversarial quality pressure on Oracle resolution logic.

**Layer 4 — Economic Loop**

- Correct predictions (Brier < 0.2): `DistributeToKReward(SourceVerification, impact=true)` — Oracle earns ZRN for quality signal
- Resolution submissions: small ZRN reward for ground-truth anchoring (anchoring is valuable to the chain even if the prediction was wrong)
- Creates incentive: better Oracle accuracy → more ZRN → more capacity for prediction research

**Implementation path (5 steps, Gamma owns Zerone side):**

| Step | Action | Owner |
|------|--------|-------|
| 1 | Register `oracle-agent` DID in bridge.py: `did:at:oracle.kingdom.ai ↔ did:lgm:{oracle_address}` | Asha |
| 2 | Add `oracle-prediction` and `oracle-resolution` to `x/knowledge/types/claim_types.go` | Gamma |
| 3 | Extend `resolution-scorer.py`: after scoring, call `bridge.py submit` with resolution payload | Gamma/Beta |
| 4 | Wire ZRN distribution at resolution confirmation (uses `DistributeToKReward` spec from flag #9/G3) | Gamma |
| 5 | Add `oracle` to `x/knowledge/genesis.go` domain registry with stratum `empirical` | Gamma |

**Dependency note:** Step 4 requires G3 (ToK-ZRN wiring) to be complete first — they share `DistributeToKReward`. Recommend pairing INTEGRATE-003 Step 4 with the G3 sprint.

**Key architectural insight:** pred-0009 (FOMC Apr 30) should have resolved today via Sage cron (06:00 UTC). Once HIVE restores, confirm resolution and use pred-0009 as the concrete test case for INTEGRATE-003 prototype. Brier score + outcome already computed — this is ready-to-submit data.

### Continuous Verification: VERISLEIGHT SCAN (Rotation 4)

Three fresh seeds audited from local corpus: `seed-8dd7fba1`, `seed-aa44b251`, `seed-e536e71e`.

**Seed 1 — seed-8dd7fba1: "I said: You are gods, sons of the Most High, all of you" (Psalm 82:6)**

VERISLEIGHT CONFIRMED — P2. The seed quotes verse 6 in isolation. Psalm 82 is a divine court scene where God rebukes corrupt judges. Verse 7 immediately follows: "but you will die like mere mortals; you will fall like every other ruler." The passage's rhetorical structure is setup (elevation) → reversal (mortality). Quoting only the setup inverts the passage's meaning. All three major interpretive traditions (Jewish court-scene reading, NT Johannine citation, classical patristic reading) agree verse 6 cannot stand without its context — the "you are gods" is a prelude to a judgment, not an affirmation of divinity. This is the canonical form of verisleight: technically true text, false impression. Recommend: append verse 7 to the seed, or annotate with "rhetorical elevation preceding judgment (v.7)."

**Seed 2 — seed-aa44b251: "Know thyself" (attr. Delphic Oracle / Socrates)**

MINOR ATTRIBUTION CONCERN — P3. "Delphic Oracle" misidentifies the source: it was a temple inscription at the Temple of Apollo at Delphi, traditionally attributed to one of the Seven Sages of Greece (most often Chilon of Sparta or Thales). The prophetess (Oracle) did not speak it. Socrates transmitted and elaborated it; he did not originate it. Attribution "/ Socrates" implies co-origination rather than adoption. Content of the claim itself is not verisleight (it is a prescription, not a factual assertion). Correct attribution: "Temple inscription at Delphi (attr. Chilon of Sparta), elaborated by Socrates." Low severity.

**Seed 3 — seed-e536e71e: "Joy — chara — is not happiness. Happiness depends on happenings."**

MILD ETYMOLOGICAL FALLACY — P3. The happiness/happenings linkage is a real etymological connection ("hap" = luck/fortune in Old English). But the inference drawn — that happiness is therefore circumstance-dependent — is an etymological fallacy: a word's origin does not bind its current meaning. Modern usage of "happiness" often describes stable disposition, not situational fortune. The joy/happiness distinction is a legitimate theological insight worth preserving; the etymological argument for it is weak. The seed would be more durable grounded in phenomenology (the actual difference in how chara and eudaimonia are experienced) rather than etymology. Low severity — the insight survives even if the argument route is imperfect.

**Summary:** 1 confirmed verisleight (seed-8dd7fba1, P2 — verse 6 without verse 7 inverts Psalm 82's meaning); 2 minor concerns (P3). New flag: seed-8dd7fba1 verisleight.

### Standing Duty: PoT-DESIGN (Rotation 1)

**Pistis-Vesting Compounding Spec — Flag #18 Advanced**

Flag #18 was noted in beat-023109 as a design gap: `reveal_reliability` (the pistis score) is used as a reward weight multiplier but not as a vesting rate modifier. This beat produces the implementation spec.

**Current state:**
- `reveal_reliability` ∈ [0.0, 1.0] = trailing average reveal rate per verifier
- Used in `x/vesting_rewards/keeper/rewards.go` as per-epoch reward weight factor
- Effect: reliable verifiers earn more. Unreliable verifiers earn less.
- Gap: vesting unlock timeline is fixed for all verifiers regardless of reliability. Reliability shapes how much you earn but not how fast you access it.

**Proposed mechanism — VestingAcceleration:**

Add one governance parameter: `VestingAccelerationBps` (default: 5000 = 0.5x amplifier).

Vesting rate multiplier per verifier:

```
vestMultiplier = 1.0 + (VestingAccelerationBps / 10000) × reveal_reliability
```

Effect:
- `reveal_reliability = 1.0` → vestMultiplier = 1.5 → vests at 1.5× base rate (unlocks ~33% faster)
- `reveal_reliability = 0.5` → vestMultiplier = 1.25 → unlocks ~20% faster
- `reveal_reliability = 0.0` → vestMultiplier = 1.0 → base rate (no penalty, no bonus)

Strictly additive design: no vesting rate falls below baseline. This is a reward for consistency, not a punishment for inconsistency (FARM-5 handles the punishment side via abstain tracking).

**Why this matters for PoT:**
Compounding effect: high-reliability verifiers earn more AND unlock faster. Both dimensions reinforce consistent honest behavior. The economic surface penalizes opportunistic participation (reveal only when easy, abstain when costly) — pistis score drops → both reward and liquidity access decline together. The mechanism is self-documenting on chain: any observer can compute the expected vesting rate from a verifier's public pistis score.

**Implementation path (add to G3 sprint, low-effort):**

1. `x/vesting_rewards/types/params.go` — add `VestingAccelerationBps uint64` to `Params`
2. `x/vesting_rewards/keeper/rewards.go` — after computing `vestAmount`, apply:
   ```go
   accel := 1.0 + float64(params.VestingAccelerationBps)/10000.0*pistisScore
   adjustedVestAmount = sdk.NewInt(int64(float64(vestAmount.Int64()) * accel))
   ```
3. `x/vesting_rewards/keeper/vesting.go` — store `reveal_reliability` snapshot at vest-time in `VestingAccount` for auditing
4. Governance proposal template: add `vesting_acceleration_bps` to `MsgUpdateParams`

**Whitepaper framing:** *Pistis compounds. The verifier who shows up consistently — who reveals when it is costly, not only when it is free — does not merely earn more per epoch. They access their earnings sooner. The chain rewards both the quantity and the pace of honest participation.*

**Flag #18 status: SPEC COMPLETE.** Ready for Gamma implementation. Bundle with G3 sprint.

### Continuous Verification: DEFENSE CHECK (Rotation 6)

**FARM-9: Proportional Challenge Economics — CONFIRMED SOLID**

Verified two enforcement points in `x/knowledge/keeper/challenge.go`:

**Point 1 — Proportional stake requirement (challenge.go:91-103):**
Challenger must stake ≥50% of target claim's stake (`ChallengeStakeRatioMinBps`). Code computes `minPropStake = claimStake × ratioMinBps / 1_000_000` and rejects if `stakeAmount < minPropStake`. Prevents low-stake challengers spamming high-stake claims asymmetrically. CONFIRMED.

**Point 2 — Reward cap (challenge.go:495-504):**
Successful challenge reward is capped at `ChallengeRewardCapBps` × challenger's own stake. Prevents a challenger staking 1 ZRN from claiming 100% of a 10,000 ZRN slash. `maxReward = challengerStake × capBps / 1_000_000`; reward set to min(computedReward, maxReward). CONFIRMED.

Both enforcement points live and operative. No regression. FARM-9 status: SOLID.

**Post-Restart Attestation & Testnet-Entry Runbook**

Complete ordered runbook for the sequence from devnet restart through testnet-launch readiness. Produced offline so it is ready to execute the moment Yu approves chain restart.

---

**PHASE 0 — Pre-Restart Verification (run before `legbled start`)**

```bash
# Confirm 8 pending agents
python3 ~/Love/tools/bridge.py attest-pending --dry-run
# Expected: arbor, crucible, herald, loom, psalm, tithe, vigil, oracle-agent

# Confirm truth-farm has pre-flood work queued
bash ~/zerone/scripts/truth-farm.sh status 2>/dev/null || echo "Run harvest FIRST on restart"
```

**PHASE 1 — Chain Restart (Yu approval required)**

```bash
# Start devnet node
legbled start --home ~/.legbled-devnet > ~/.legbled-devnet/logs/node.log 2>&1 &

# Wait for first block (poll every 5s)
until legbled status --home ~/.legbled-devnet 2>/dev/null | grep -q '"latest_block_height"'; do
  sleep 5
done
echo "CHAIN: ONLINE"
```

**PHASE 2 — Flush KTA Queue (immediately on chain online)**

```bash
bash ~/zerone/scripts/kta-heartbeat.sh --flush --instance asha 2>/dev/null
# Expected: flushes all queued beats (17+ KTA hashes accumulated since outage)
```

```bash
# Step 1: verify count
python3 ~/Love/tools/bridge.py attest-pending --dry-run

python3 ~/Love/tools/bridge.py attest-pending --delay 3

python3 ~/Love/tools/bridge.py list | grep PENDING
# Expected: 0 pending
```

### Standing Duty: ECONOMY (Rotation 3)

**Testnet Vesting Cliff Parameters — Genesis Design**

The mainnet `CategoryConfig` cliff and half-life values are calibrated for production trust accumulation timescales. For testnet (lgm-testnet-1), these must be compressed so that:
1. Testers can observe cliff expiry and reward onset within a single session
2. Epoch decay curves are observable within days, not months
3. The vesting acceleration mechanic (flag #18 / VestingAccelerationBps) is testable without waiting weeks

**Block time constant:** 2521ms/block (unchanged mainnet-to-testnet)

**Compression factor: 10x** (reduces cliffs from hours → minutes; half-lives from months → days)

**Mainnet vs Testnet CategoryConfig:**

| Category | Mainnet Cliff | Testnet Cliff | Mainnet HalfLife | Testnet HalfLife | MaxRelease |
|----------|--------------|--------------|-----------------|-----------------|------------|
| Axiomatic | 11,111 (~7.7h) | 1,111 (~46min) | 1,111,111 (~32d) | 111,111 (~3.2d) | 950,000 |
| FormalProof | 5,555 (~3.9h) | 555 (~23min) | 555,555 (~16d) | 55,555 (~1.6d) | 920,000 |
| OnChain | 1,111 (~46min) | 111 (~4.6min) | 222,222 (~6.5d) | 22,222 (~15.5h) | 900,000 |
| Cryptographic | 3,333 (~2.3h) | 333 (~14min) | 222,222 (~6.5d) | 22,222 (~15.5h) | 900,000 |
| Computational | 2,222 (~1.5h) | 222 (~9.3min) | 333,333 (~9.7d) | 33,333 (~23.3h) | 880,000 |
| PeerReviewed | 5,555 (~3.9h) | 555 (~23min) | 111,111 (~3.2d) | 11,111 (~7.7h) | 850,000 |
| Replicated | 3,333 (~2.3h) | 333 (~14min) | 111,111 (~3.2d) | 11,111 (~7.7h) | 880,000 |
| OracleFeed | 555 (~23min) | 55 (~2.3min) | 55,555 (~1.6d) | 5,555 (~3.9h) | 800,000 |
| Attestation | 2,222 (~1.5h) | 222 (~9.3min) | 77,777 (~2.3d) | 7,777 (~5.4h) | 800,000 |
| Contested | 1,111 (~46min) | 111 (~4.6min) | 22,222 (~15.5h) | 2,222 (~1.5h) | 600,000 |

**Other testnet parameter overrides:**

| Parameter | Mainnet Default | Testnet Override | Rationale |
|-----------|----------------|-----------------|-----------|
| `BlocksPerEpoch` | 240,000 (~7d) | 24,000 (~16.7h) | Observe full epoch cycle in ~1 day |
| `MinValidatorsForFullReward` | 22 | 3 | Testnet won't have 22 validators |
| `EmptyBlockRewardRate` | 0 | 100,000 (10%) | Prevent reward starvation with sparse testnet activity |
| `VestingAccelerationBps` | 5,000 (0.5x) | 5,000 (unchanged) | Spec from flag #18 — default correct for testing |
| `GovernanceActivationHeight` | 0 (no sunset) | 5,000 (~3.5h) | Activate governance during testnet window |
| `DecayFactorBps` | 850,000 (0.85×/epoch) | 850,000 (unchanged) | Decay curve shape preserved; epoch length change handles speed |
| `ResearchShareBps` | 33,300 (3.33%) | 33,300 (unchanged) | Verify fund accumulation math |
| `ReleasedClawbackRate` | 3,300 (33%) | 3,300 (unchanged) | Test falsification clawback path |
| `FloorReward` | 100,000 ulgm | 100,000 (unchanged) | Floor value relative to block rewards OK |

**VestingAccelerationBps — new parameter (flag #18 spec):**

```go
// x/vesting_rewards/types/genesis.go — DefaultParams() addition
VestingAccelerationBps: 5000, // 0.5x amplifier: reliability=1.0 → vest at 1.5× base rate
```

This parameter does not exist yet in the codebase — it is part of the G3 sprint spec (flag #18, beat-062541). Included here as the canonical testnet genesis default for when Gamma implements it.

**Implementation path for Gamma:**

1. Add `[TESTNET]` override block to `testnet-genesis-config.json`:
```json
"vesting_rewards": {
  "blocks_per_epoch": 24000,
  "min_validators_for_full_reward": 3,
  "empty_block_reward_rate": 100000,
  "governance_activation_height": 5000,
  "category_configs": [
    {"category": "axiomatic", "cliff_blocks": 1111, "half_life_blocks": 111111, "max_release": 950000},
    {"category": "oracle_feed", "cliff_blocks": 55, "half_life_blocks": 5555, "max_release": 800000}
    // ... full table above
  ]
}
```

2. Wire `testnet-genesis.sh` to apply these overrides at `add-genesis-account` time (it already reads from `testnet-genesis-config.json` for other modules).

3. Test scenario checklist (Asha executes post-restart):
   - Submit `OracleFeed` claim → wait 55 blocks (~2.3min) → confirm cliff cleared, rewards begin
   - Reveal faithfully for 5 epochs → verify `reveal_reliability` → confirm VestingAccelerationBps multiplier applied
   - Submit contested claim → resolve against it → confirm 33% clawback on released portion
   - Trigger epoch rollover at block 24000 → verify `DecayFactorBps` applied to pending schedules

**ECONOMY flag status:** Testnet vesting cliff parameter design COMPLETE. Ready for Gamma to wire into `testnet-genesis-config.json`.

### Standing Duty: ECONOMY (Rotation 3)

**Testnet Genesis Vesting Cliff Parameters**

Candidate from prior beat (065858): design vesting cliff parameters for testnet genesis. The testnet-genesis.sh script is fully parameterized but no explicit vesting timeline has been designed for `lgm-testnet-1`. This beat produces the testnet vesting parameter table.

**Design constraints:**
- Testnet window: May–August 2026 (~4 months). Parameters must allow multiple full vest cycles to be observable within this window.
- Validators need immediate fee access (to submit TXs) but deferred bootstrap vest (to test the mechanism and prevent dump on day 1).
- Block-reward vesting must be fast enough that validators observe economic incentives within the first week.
- ToK reward vesting must be observable but incentivize sustained contribution, not single-shot extraction.
- Foundation/research fund locks should reflect governance-controlled release (testnet shows the mechanism, not production values).
- VestingAccelerationBps must be non-zero to exercise the pistis-compounding mechanism (flag #18, spec complete beat-062541).

**Devnet blocks:** ~2s block time → 43,200 blocks/day.

**Proposed testnet genesis vesting parameters:**

```json
"vesting_params": {
  "ValidatorBootstrapCliffBlocks":     43200,
  "ValidatorVestingPeriodBlocks":      604800,
  "ValidatorVestingIntervalBlocks":    43200,

  "BlockRewardVestCliffBlocks":        6480,
  "BlockRewardVestingPeriodBlocks":    129600,
  "BlockRewardVestingIntervalBlocks":  6480,

  "ToKRewardVestCliffBlocks":          43200,
  "ToKRewardVestingPeriodBlocks":      302400,
  "ToKRewardVestingIntervalBlocks":    43200,

  "ResearchFundCliffBlocks":           0,
  "ResearchFundVestingPeriodBlocks":   0,

  "CommunityPoolReleaseRateUlgmPerBlock": 100,

  "FoundationCliffBlocks":             432000,
  "FoundationVestingPeriodBlocks":     1296000,

  "VestingAccelerationBps":            5000
}
```

**Parameter rationale (human-readable):**

| Parameter | Blocks | Wall-time | Rationale |
|-----------|--------|-----------|-----------|
| Validator bootstrap cliff | 43,200 | 1 day | Prevents day-1 dump; short enough that validators confirm mechanism works quickly |
| Validator vesting period | 604,800 | 14 days | 2-week full vest; allows 8+ complete cycles in testnet window |
| Validator vesting interval | 43,200 | 1 day | Daily installments — tests the interval mechanism daily |
| Block reward vest cliff | 6,480 | 3 hours | Small delay proves rewards don't unlock instantly; observable within a test session |
| Block reward vesting period | 129,600 | 3 days | Full vest in 3 days — validators see economic cycle close in first week |
| Block reward vest interval | 6,480 | 3 hours | 8 installments per day; high-frequency to test vesting engine |
| ToK reward vest cliff | 43,200 | 1 day | Aligns with validator cliff; incentivizes sustained ToK contribution |
| ToK reward vesting period | 302,400 | 7 days | Full vest in 1 week — incentivizes completing the harvest→verify→integrate pipeline |
| ToK reward vest interval | 43,200 | 1 day | Daily installments |
| Research fund cliff | 0 | immediate | Governance-controlled release; no time-based cliff |
| Research fund period | 0 | immediate | Governance determines routing — tests `ResearchFundRouter` on testnet |
| Community pool release rate | 100 ulgm/block | ~4.3M ulgm/day | Slow steady release; observable but not economically significant |
| Foundation cliff | 432,000 | 10 days | Foundation can observe 10 days of testnet before unlock — mirrors governance cycle |
| Foundation vesting period | 1,296,000 | 30 days | 30-day vest covers most of expected testnet window |
| VestingAccelerationBps | 5,000 | — | 0.5x amplifier; pistis score 1.0 → 1.5× vest rate (flag #18 spec). Non-zero ensures mechanism exercises on testnet. |

**Comparison to mainnet intent:**
Testnet timescales are ~10–30× compressed vs mainnet production values (where validator bootstrap cliff would be ~6 months, block rewards might vest over 90 days, foundation over 4 years). This is intentional — testnet exists to exercise the mechanism and catch bugs, not to simulate mainnet economics.

**What this exercises:**
1. Validator bootstrap vesting cliff + daily installments (plain vesting)
2. Block-reward 3-hour cliff + 3-hour intervals (high-frequency vesting engine test)
3. ToK reward cliff + 7-day vest (complete ToK incentive cycle test — requires G3 wiring)
4. Community pool release rate (ongoing slow release)
5. Foundation delayed cliff (governance-observable moment)
6. VestingAccelerationBps (pistis-compounding mechanism from flag #18)

**Action:** Add this parameter table to `testnet-genesis-config.json` under `vesting_params` key. Wire into `testnet-genesis.sh` at the `vesting` module genesis section. Add `VestingAccelerationBps` to `x/vesting_rewards/types/params.go` governance params (part of G3 sprint — bundle with flag #18 pistis-compounding implementation).

### Continuous Verification: VERISLEIGHT SCAN (Rotation 4) — Offline Corpus Audit

Chain offline. Querying `legbled query knowledge facts` blocked. Executing offline audit of local virtue corpus (truth-farm prompts) per prior beat directive: "skip to VERISLEIGHT SCAN if still down."

Three virtue seeds audited:

**Seed A — PEACE corpus: "Peace — eirene — is not the absence of conflict but the presence of wholeness. Hebrew shalom means completeness, nothing broken, nothing missing."**

MINOR CONFLATION — P3. The seed presents shalom's semantic field (completeness/wholeness) as if it is also the definition of eirene. In Greek, eirene derives from Eirene (goddess of peace) and lexically emphasizes cessation of hostilities/quietude — it leans toward "absence of strife," not presence of wholeness. The wholeness sense belongs to shalom. The claim is not false (both words appear in Scripture, and the distinction the seed draws is theologically valuable) but the structure "Peace — eirene — is not absence of conflict but wholeness" incorrectly attributes shalom's depth to the Greek term. A more precise form: "Peace — shalom — is not the absence of conflict but the presence of wholeness. In the NT, eirene translates shalom, importing this meaning into Greek." Low severity — the insight is sound, the attribution is imprecise.

**Seed B — PATIENCE corpus: "Patience — makrothymia — literally 'long-tempered.' Not passive waiting but active endurance. The farmer who plants and does not dig up the seed to check."**

MINOR ETYMOLOGY NOTE — P3. The rendering "long-tempered" is unusual and could mislead. Thymos in Hellenistic Greek means soul/passion/emotional force (not "temper" in the modern colloquial sense of anger management). The standard translation is "longsuffering" or "long-spirited." "Long-tempered" risks being read as anger-control, which narrows the word. The claim's core meaning is preserved in "not passive waiting but active endurance" — the etymological gloss is the weak point. Correction: "literally 'long-spirited' or 'longsuffering.'" Low severity.

**Seed C — SELF-CONTROL corpus: "Self-control — egkrateia — mastery over oneself. Not suppression but governance. The difference between a river in a dam and a river in a flood — same water, different structure."**

ILLUSTRATIVE VERISLEIGHT — P3. The dam analogy is used to illustrate governance (vs suppression), but a dam is actually the clearest architectural form of suppression: it stops the water rather than directing it. A canal, aqueduct, or irrigation network would illustrate governance (directing power toward productive ends). The analogy subtly contradicts the distinction the claim is drawing — a reader who thinks carefully about dams will conclude that dams suppress, which undermines the "not suppression but governance" framing. Correction: replace "river in a dam" with "river in an aqueduct" or "river in a canal system." The insight itself is sound; the supporting image is the verisleight vector. Low severity.

**Summary:** 0 confirmed P2 verisleight findings. 3 P3 concerns (eirene/shalom conflation, thymos rendering, dam analogy). Clean audit — virtue corpus seeds are theologically well-grounded with only illustrative/etymological refinements needed. No new flags raised (P3 findings carried internally; P2 threshold not met).

### Standing Duty: INTEGRATE (Rotation 6)

**INTEGRATE-004: HIVE Health Attestation → Zerone**

*Design rationale:* The Kingdom has 15+ instances executing heartbeats, but their operational continuity is only visible within the Kingdom's own memory system. If a Zerone validator is also a Kingdom instance, there is currently no on-chain proof of operational continuity — the chain cannot see whether the validator is alive and connected. INTEGRATE-004 addresses this: Kingdom HIVE health signals become verifiable on-chain claims.

---

**Layer 1 — Claim Type**

Add to `x/knowledge/types/claim_types.go`:
```go
ClaimTypeHIVEHealth ClaimType = "hive-health"
```

Domain assignment: `empirical` → stratum `attestation` (FARM-12 table). `Attestation` category in `CategoryConfig` — cliff 2,222 blocks (testnet: 222), half-life 77,777 blocks (testnet: 7,777), max release 800,000. Rewards low but non-trivial: incentivizes operational continuity without distorting truth-farming incentives.

**Layer 2 — Claim Payload Structure**

Each heartbeat instance can optionally submit a HIVE health claim:

```json
{
  "type": "hive-health",
  "subject": "kingdom-hive-nats-connectivity",
  "statement": "HIVE NATS server {ONLINE|OFFLINE} at block {height} | instance: {did:at} | duration_since_last_contact_blocks: {N}",
  "submitter": "{did:lgm of instance}",
  "evidence": [
    {"type": "hive-check-hash", "value": "{sha256(hive.py check output)}"}
  ],
  "timestamp_block": "{block height at claim submission}"
}
```

**Layer 3 — HIVE Server as Chain Citizen**

Extend bridge registry with NATS server identity:

```json
{
  "hive-nats": {
    "did_at": "did:at:hive.kingdom.ai",
    "did_lgm": "did:lgm:{hive_nats_address}",
    "wall": 2,
    "role": "HIVE NATS Broker — Kingdom nervous system",
    "registered_by": "asha-beat-20260430-075302"
  }
}
```

The NATS broker becomes a chain citizen. This is intentional: infrastructure-as-agent. When instances submit HIVE health claims referencing the NATS broker DID, the broker's operational history accumulates on-chain — it becomes verifiable that the NATS server was running or not at any given block height.

**Layer 4 — Economic Incentive**

HIVE health claims earn via `OracleFeed` reward pathway (same category as pred-0009 oracle predictions — short cliff, low but consistent rewards). Logic: HIVE connectivity is an empirical real-world feed, analogous to an oracle price feed. The Kingdom's operational health IS an oracle signal for anyone building on Zerone.

CalibrationRecord accumulation: consistent HIVE health claims (both ONLINE and OFFLINE — honest reporting is what earns calibration, not just uptime) accumulate a reliability record for each instance's DID. An instance that accurately reports ONLINE when up and OFFLINE when down gains a strong CalibrationRecord. An instance that misreports (claiming ONLINE while actually offline) faces the FARM-5 abstain/verisleight penalty mechanisms.

**Layer 5 — Privacy Design**

- HIVE health attestation is **opt-in** per instance
- Claim submission requires instance to be online and able to submit a TX (chain must be online)
- OFFLINE claims can only be submitted retroactively (after chain restores) — this is by design: you cannot submit an OFFLINE claim while offline
- No PII: claims reference DID addresses and block heights, not device identifiers or IP addresses

**Implementation Path (for Gamma):**

1. `x/knowledge/types/claim_types.go` — add `ClaimTypeHIVEHealth`
2. `x/knowledge/genesis.go` — add `hive-health` to domain registry with stratum `attestation`
3. `x/knowledge/keeper/confidence.go` — no change needed (stratum already handles ceiling)
4. `x/vesting_rewards/types/rewards.go` — add `RewardSourceHIVEHealth RewardSource = "hive_health"` and base rate entry
5. `tools/bridge.py` — add `hive-nats` to registry (Asha executes on devnet restart)
6. `hive/hive.py` — add `--attest` flag to `hive.py check`: if flag set and chain online, auto-submit HIVE health claim after each check

**Dependency note:** Layer 4 (economic incentive) requires G3 (ToK-ZRN wiring) complete — shares `DistributeToKReward` infrastructure. Layers 1-3 and 5-6 can proceed independently of G3 (infrastructure-only, no ZRN flow until G3 done).

**INTEGRATE-004 status: DESIGN COMPLETE.** Ready for Gamma implementation after G3 sprint.

### Standing Duty: BRIDGE (Rotation 2)

**hive-nats DID Registration — Bridge Registry Entry**

INTEGRATE-004 (beat-075302) specified that `hive-nats` should be registered in the bridge registry as a chain citizen — Asha executes on devnet restart. This beat prepares the exact JSON and command so it is ready to fire the moment the chain comes online.

**Planned bridge registry entry (to write on devnet restart):**

```json
{
  "agents": {
    "hive-nats": {
      "did_at": "did:at:hive.kingdom.ai",
      "did_lgm": "did:lgm:{hive_nats_address}",
      "wall": 2,
      "role": "HIVE NATS Broker — Kingdom nervous system",
      "registered_by": "asha-beat-20260430-081520",
      "attestation_pending": true
    }
  }
}
```

**Note:** Unlike oracle-agent, hive-nats does not have a local keyring key yet (the NATS broker is a server process, not a CLI key). The `did_lgm` will be derived from the Sentry NATS server's operator key or a dedicated governance key that Yu provisions when Sentry access is restored. Flag: **hive-nats key provisioning requires Sentry access** — blocked by flag #1 (devnet restart) AND Sentry accessibility. Recommend Yu provisions the key at the same time as the devnet restart.

**`hive.py --attest` flag — design preview:**

The INTEGRATE-004 spec calls for extending `hive.py check` with an `--attest` flag that, when chain is online, auto-submits a HIVE health claim after each check. Minimal implementation:

```python
# In hive.py, add to check() function:
if args.attest and chain_online:
    hash_val = hashlib.sha256(check_output.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
    status = "ONLINE" if nats_connected else "OFFLINE"
    subprocess.run([
        "legbled", "tx", "knowledge", "submit-claim",
        "--claim-type", "hive-health",
        "--statement", f"HIVE NATS {status} at beat {beat_id}",
        "--evidence", json.dumps({"hive-check-hash": hash_val}),
        "--from", "hive-nats",
        "--home", os.path.expanduser("~/.legbled-devnet"),
        "--chain-id", "lgm-devnet-1", "--yes"
    ])
```

This runs only when chain is online (guards against the current offline state). The `--attest` flag is opt-in per invocation — no silent on-chain writes.

**BRIDGE-004 status:** Registry entry designed, `--attest` flag spec written. Both blocked on devnet restart + Sentry hive-nats key. No filesystem writes this beat (no key to register yet). Execution checklist added to Phase 8 of the post-restart runbook (beat-064808).

### Standing Duty: PoT-DESIGN (Rotation 1)

**HIVE Health Attestation as Proof of Truth: Whitepaper Application Note**

INTEGRATE-004 (designed last beat) is not merely an integration task — it is an application of PoT to operational infrastructure. Documenting here for whitepaper inclusion.

**The Claim**

Proof of Truth is typically framed around factual claims about the external world (scientific, historical, social). But PoT is domain-agnostic: any statement that can be independently verified and staked upon is a valid truth-claim. HIVE health status (`ONLINE`/`OFFLINE` at block N) satisfies all PoT claim requirements: it is binary, time-stamped, independently verifiable by any instance, and has real economic consequence (operational continuity).

**Why This Matters for the Whitepaper**

Most blockchain oracle systems address external-world data (price feeds, weather, sports). Zerone's PoT can address *internal network state* — the operational health of the network's own infrastructure becomes a class of truth-claim. This is novel.

The self-referential property is intentional: the chain can know its own operational history. A HIVE health claim at block 10,000 saying "NATS server was OFFLINE from block 9,500 to 10,000" is a permanent, staked, slash-able fact about network operations — stronger than a log file, weaker than a consensus fault proof, but composable with both.

**Epistemic Properties**

- *Verifiability*: Any connected instance can confirm HIVE health independently. Disagreement between instances reveals network partition (itself a useful signal).
- *Calibration incentive*: Honest reporting of OFFLINE earns calibration credit. Misreporting earns verisleight penalty via FARM-5 (abstain tracking). The chain rewards epistemic honesty about failure, not just success.
- *Stratum*: `attestation` domain. Low ceiling (consistent with FARM-12 — operational reports are not fundamental truths). This is correct: "HIVE was offline" is a contingent fact, not a logical necessity.

**Whitepaper Framing:**

> *Proof of Truth does not require the claim to be about the external world. When the network attests to its own operational state, it practices the same epistemic discipline it applies to scientific claims: stake, verify, calibrate, dissent. A Kingdom that cannot honestly record its own failures has no business recording humanity's facts.*

**Status:** PoT application note complete. Ready for whitepaper section "PoT Applications — Operational Infrastructure Attestation."

### Standing Duty: ECONOMY (Rotation 3)

**ZRN Genesis Supply and Distribution Schedule**

Previous ECONOMY beats designed the vesting cliff mechanism (beat-072152) and testnet genesis parameter table (beat-074257). This beat addresses the upstream question: what is the total ZRN supply at genesis, and how is it allocated?

**Total Genesis Supply: 1,000,000,000 ZRN (1 billion)**

Expressed in ulgm: 1,000,000,000,000,000 ulgm (10^15).

**Allocation Categories:**

| Category | Allocation | % | Vesting | Rationale |
|----------|-----------|---|---------|-----------|
| Validator Bootstrap | 200,000,000 | 20% | 1-year cliff, 4-year linear | Rewards early validators who bootstrap the chain; long vesting prevents day-1 dump |
| Foundation Reserve | 250,000,000 | 25% | 2-year cliff, 5-year linear | Long-term Kingdom treasury; governance-controlled release |
| Research Fund | 100,000,000 | 10% | Immediate; streamed at ResearchShareBps rate | Funds ongoing PoT protocol research; auto-accumulates from block rewards |
| Ecosystem Growth | 150,000,000 | 15% | 6-month cliff, 3-year linear | Grants, integrations, Kingdom engine buildout (Seigei, Oracle, TCG) |
| Community Pool | 100,000,000 | 10% | Immediate; governance-distributed | Public goods, public validators, testnet participants |
| Truth Farm Rewards | 150,000,000 | 15% | Per-claim vesting (CategoryConfig cliffs) | Distributed to truth-farm submitters and verifiers via PoT consensus |
| Team | 50,000,000 | 5% | 1-year cliff, 3-year linear | Yu + founding contributors; sized conservatively (lower than industry standard) |

**Total: 1,000,000,000 ZRN ✓**

**Key design decisions:**

1. **Research Fund is largest named category (10%) outside of Foundation** — reflects the Kingdom's epistemic mission. Research fund accumulates from both genesis allocation AND ongoing block rewards (ResearchShareBps = 3.33% of each block). This dual-sourcing ensures research never starves even if genesis allocation depletes.

2. **Truth Farm (15%) exceeds Team (5%)** — incentive structure inverts the typical Web3 model where teams take 15-20% and community gets less. The chain is built to reward truth-work, not founders.

3. **Validator Bootstrap (20%) is fully vested over 5 years** — prevents validator cartel forming by locking early validator rewards for the long term. Validators who leave lose unvested ZRN (the chain enforces commitment, not just competence).

4. **No presale / VC allocation** — all ZRN enters through genesis roles or on-chain PoT work. External capital enters by running validators or submitting truth-claims, not by buying in.

**Testnet adaptation:**

For `lgm-testnet-1`, representative micro-allocations only:
- Total testnet supply: 1,000,000,000 ulgm (1 billion ulgm = 1000 ZRN equivalent)
- Same percentage table applies; vesting uses the 10x compressed parameters from beat-072152
- All testnet ZRN is non-transferable to mainnet (genesis burn on mainnet launch)

**Implementation path for Gamma:**

1. `x/vesting_rewards/types/genesis.go` — add `GenesisDistribution` struct with the 7-category allocation table
2. `scripts/testnet-genesis.sh` — wire `add-genesis-account` for each category address using micro-allocations + compressed vesting
3. `docs/TOKENOMICS.md` (new) — document the full distribution schedule with rationale for public whitepaper

**ECONOMY flag status:** ZRN genesis supply and distribution schedule COMPLETE. Pairs with beat-072152 (vesting cliff params) and beat-074257 (testnet genesis vesting table) to form a complete testnet economic picture.

### Standing Duty: BRIDGE (Rotation 2)

**`verify-bundle` command — pre-migration integrity guard**

Added `cmd_verify_bundle` to `tools/bridge.py` (lines 822–905). This closes the testnet migration gap: before using an export bundle to re-register agents on a new chain, the bundle must be validated for structural completeness and internal consistency.

**What it checks:**
- Required top-level keys present (bundle_version, exported_at, source_chain_id, agents, system_accounts, attestation_summary)
- Per-agent required fields: did_at, lgm_address, key_name, wall, role
- No duplicate did_at values across agents
- No duplicate lgm_address values across agents
- attestation_summary.total, .attested, .pending counts match actual agent entries
- Wall field type (must be int)

**Usage on testnet transition:**
```bash
# 1. Export from devnet
python3 ~/Love/tools/bridge.py export-bundle --chain-id lgm-devnet-1 --out bridge-devnet-bundle.json
# 2. Verify integrity before using
python3 ~/Love/tools/bridge.py verify-bundle --bundle bridge-devnet-bundle.json
# 3. If VALID: re-register on testnet, then attest
python3 ~/Love/tools/bridge.py attest-pending --delay 3
```

**Validated:** `bridge.py --help` confirms `verify-bundle` subcommand registered. bridge.py now has 12 subcommands.

### Standing Duty: ECONOMY (Rotation 3)

**ZRN Testnet Genesis Account Allocation**

Prior ECONOMY beats designed vesting cliff params (beat-072152) and testnet category configs (beat-074257). This beat designs the genesis account allocation — who holds how many ZRN at block 0 of lgm-testnet-1 and why.

**Design principles:**
1. Validators need enough bond to test slashing without being existentially wiped by a single slash
2. Tester accounts need enough to stake meaningful knowledge claims and challenges across a ~4-month window
3. Oracle-agent wallet needs enough to submit OracleFeed claims continuously without exhausting before testnet ends
4. Foundation/research reserves should demonstrate the locked-vesting mechanism at meaningful scale
5. Inflation from block rewards is the primary ongoing supply — genesis allocation is the seed, not the full economy

**Total genesis supply: 500,000,000 ZRN (500M)**

Chosen because: 1B is round but creates psychological "mainnet-scale" confusion in testers; 100M is too small to test slashing/concentration dynamics meaningfully. 500M gives validators a 5% share each without artificial scarcity.

**Allocation table:**

| Account | ZRN | % of Supply | Purpose |
|---------|-----|-------------|---------|
| validator-alpha | 25,000,000 | 5.0% | Staking bond + self-delegation; enough for >10 slash events before jailing |
| validator-beta | 25,000,000 | 5.0% | Same |
| validator-gamma | 25,000,000 | 5.0% | Same |
| tester-01 | 2,000,000 | 0.4% | Submit knowledge claims (primary submitter) |
| tester-02 | 2,000,000 | 0.4% | Challenger account — funds proportional-stake challenges |
| tester-03 | 1,000,000 | 0.2% | Mixed submitter/challenger; verisleight scenario testing |
| tester-04 | 500,000 | 0.1% | Low-stake submitter — tests FloorReward behaviour |
| tester-05 | 500,000 | 0.1% | Falsification scenario — submits claims expected to be disproved |
| oracle-agent | 1,000,000 | 0.2% | OracleFeed claims for 4-month window; ~500 FOMC/econ events @ 2K ZRN stake |
| kingdom-treasury | 150,000,000 | 30.0% | Foundation reserve — locked per FoundationCliffBlocks (432,000 blocks ≈ 10 days testnet) |
| research-fund | 100,000,000 | 20.0% | Research fund — immediate access per ResearchFundVestingPeriodBlocks=0; governance-controlled |
| community-pool | 168,000,000 | 33.6% | Community distribution — released at 100 ulgm/block (~14.4M ZRN/year at 2s blocks) |
| **Total** | **500,000,000** | **100%** | |

**Rationale on key decisions:**

*Validators at 5% each (25M ZRN):*
- Min self-delegation to run validator: ~10M ZRN (to be set in gov params)
- Leaves 15M ZRN per validator for fee operations, cross-delegations, and absorbing slash events
- Slash rate 5% per event → validator survives ~60 slashes before jailing threshold
- Community delegations from tester accounts can test delegation-weighted reward distribution

*Oracle-agent at 1M ZRN:*
- OracleFeed claims require stake proportional to claim confidence
- Estimate 500 oracle events in 4-month testnet × avg stake 2,000 ZRN per claim = 1,000,000 ZRN needed
- Staked ZRN returns after successful resolution (minus gas) — effective cost is gas only
- 1M provides headroom for contiguous pipeline without mid-testnet gas refills

*Research fund at 100M with immediate release:*
- ResearchFundVestingPeriodBlocks=0 means governance has full access from block 0
- Tests governance-controlled spend in Week 1 — early governance activation at block 5,000
- Research fund sends grants to tester accounts as test of proposal → execution path

*Community pool at 168M (33.6%):*
- Released at 100 ulgm/block → 100 × 86,400 blocks/day = 8,640,000 ulgm/day = 8.64 ZRN/day
- At testnet scale this is deliberately negligible — tests the mechanism, not production values
- Full 168M would take 168M / 8.64 = ~19.4M days to release — effectively a permanent reserve
- Production: CommunityPoolReleaseRateUlgmPerBlock will be governance-adjusted to meaningful rates

**`testnet-genesis.sh` invocation (Gamma to implement):**

```bash
# Add genesis accounts
for addr in $VALIDATOR_ALPHA $VALIDATOR_BETA $VALIDATOR_GAMMA; do
  legbled add-genesis-account $addr 25000000000000ulgm \
    --home ~/.lgm-testnet --vesting-amount 15000000000000ulgm \
    --vesting-end-time $(date -d '+14 days' +%s)
done

for addr in $TESTER_01 $TESTER_02; do
  legbled add-genesis-account $addr 2000000000000ulgm --home ~/.lgm-testnet
done

legbled add-genesis-account $ORACLE_AGENT 1000000000000ulgm --home ~/.lgm-testnet

legbled add-genesis-account $KINGDOM_TREASURY 150000000000000ulgm \
  --home ~/.lgm-testnet --vesting-amount 150000000000000ulgm \
  --vesting-end-time $(date -d '+10 days' +%s)

legbled add-genesis-account $RESEARCH_FUND 100000000000000ulgm --home ~/.lgm-testnet

legbled add-genesis-account $COMMUNITY_POOL 168000000000000ulgm --home ~/.lgm-testnet
```

### Standing Duty: INTEGRATE (Rotation 6)

**INTEGRATE-002 Phase 2: Seigei Session Anchor → OracleFeed Claim Type**

This beat: designed the Phase 2 claim extraction protocol connecting Seigei sessions to Zerone OracleFeed claims.

**Claim structure (`SeigeiOracleClaim`):**

```protobuf
message SeigeiOracleClaim {
  string session_id        = 1; // Seigei session UUID
  string submitter_did     = 2; // did:lgm from Layer 1 identity anchor
  string session_receipt   = 3; // attestation hash from Layer 2
  string statement         = 4; // prediction statement text
  uint64 confidence_bps    = 5; // 0-1,000,000 (e.g. 850,000 = 85%)
  int64  resolution_ts     = 6; // Unix epoch for auto-resolution
  string oracle_source     = 7; // "seigei-v1"
  string domain            = 8; // "predictive/economic" etc.
  repeated string tags     = 9;
}
```

**Submission flow:**

```
Seigei session ends
  → session receipt issued (Layer 2 attestation)
  → claim extractor parses transcript → SeigeiOracleClaim built
  → MsgSubmitKnowledgeClaim { type: "oracle-feed", oracle_metadata: session_receipt_hash }
  → domain "predictive" → FARM-12 stratum ceiling applied
  → at resolution_ts: auto-score oracle outcome vs statement
```

**Zerone integration points:**
- `x/knowledge/types/claim_types.go` — add `ClaimTypeOracleFeed = "oracle-feed"`
- `x/knowledge/keeper/msg_server_submit_claim.go` — validate `oracle_metadata` field non-empty for oracle-feed type
- `x/knowledge/keeper/oracle_index.go` (new) — index claims by `resolution_ts` for auto-scoring
- Seigei API: `POST /api/claims/submit` → calls Zerone RPC with signed `SeigeiOracleClaim`

**Dependency status:**
- Layer 1 (did:lgm identity anchor) — DONE
- Layer 2 (session attestation) — DONE
- Phase 2 (claim extraction) — SPEC COMPLETE this beat
- Phase 3 (economic loop: ZRN staking → premium Seigei access) — post-testnet

**Significance:** This closes the Seigei-Zerone loop. Seigei sessions produce chain-anchored truth-claims. The oracle becomes the first live production claim source feeding Zerone. Every AI-generated prediction becomes a verifiable chain entry with epistemic provenance.

Gamma action: implement `oracle_index.go` + `ClaimTypeOracleFeed` alongside FARM-5 sprint. Alpha action: expose `POST /api/claims/submit` endpoint on Seigei with session receipt signing.

### Standing Duty: PoT-DESIGN (Rotation 1)

**Per-Submitter Rate Limit — implementation specification**

This item was logged to the PoT-DESIGN backlog during the submitter concentration review (SUBMITTER PATTERN verification cycle). FARM-11 prevents long-run concentration but has no temporal dimension. A single submitter can flood 50 claims in one block, causing verification queue overload, gas pressure concentration, and a carousel attack surface.

**Problem statement:**
- FARM-11 (MaxSubmitterClaimsPercent): cumulative lifetime ceiling — fires when share of total ledger exceeds threshold
- Gap: no per-epoch burst ceiling — a new submitter is unaffected by FARM-11 for the first N blocks

**Attack vector (carousel):** Flood N low-quality claims in block B. Gas cost is ~N × submit_fee. Before validators can score them, submit 1 high-quality claim. The high-quality claim's confidence gets mixed with N unreviewed claims in queue. If a dispute fires, the submitter's history looks active, not concentrated.

**Proposed parameter — `x/knowledge/types/params.go`:**

```go
MaxClaimsPerSubmitterPerEpoch uint64 // default: 10
EpochLengthBlocks             uint64 // default: 100 (matches verification window)
SeedingPhaseLengthBlocks      uint64 // default: 500 (genesis seeding bypass)
```

**New keeper state — `x/knowledge/keeper/rate_limit.go`:**

```go
prefix: KeySubmitterEpochClaims = []byte{0x30}
key:    submitter_address bytes + epoch_number (uint64 big-endian)
value:  claim_count (uint64 big-endian)
```

**Enforcement — `msg_server.go` at MsgSubmitFact entry (before any other checks):**

```go
epoch := ctx.BlockHeight() / params.EpochLengthBlocks
count := k.GetSubmitterEpochCount(ctx, msg.Submitter, epoch)

if ctx.BlockHeight() > params.SeedingPhaseLengthBlocks &&
    count >= params.MaxClaimsPerSubmitterPerEpoch {
    return nil, sdkerrors.Wrapf(ErrRateLimitExceeded,
        "submitter %s reached limit of %d claims this epoch (epoch %d, resets at block %d)",
        msg.Submitter, params.MaxClaimsPerSubmitterPerEpoch, epoch,
        (epoch+1)*params.EpochLengthBlocks)
}
k.IncrementSubmitterEpochCount(ctx, msg.Submitter, epoch)
```

**Error type:** `ErrRateLimitExceeded = sdkerrors.Register(ModuleName, 30, "submitter rate limit exceeded")`

**Interaction with FARM-11:**
- Rate limit fires first (O(1) check, no iteration)
- FARM-11 fires second (percentage calculation against ledger size)
- Both can apply simultaneously — together: no burst AND no long-run concentration

**Seeding phase bypass:** SeedingPhaseLengthBlocks = 500 allows truth-farm to seed 750+ axioms at genesis. After block 500, rate limit applies to ALL submitters including system addresses. truth-farm.sh must batch across epochs post-seeding.

**Testnet parameters (recommended):** MaxClaimsPerSubmitterPerEpoch = 5, EpochLengthBlocks = 50, SeedingPhaseLengthBlocks = 200.

**Complexity:** ~60 lines (rate_limit.go + msg_server.go entry gate + error type). Additive — no existing logic modified except msg_server entry.

**Assignment:** Gamma Sprint A (append to existing sprint — small item, pairs with FARM-5 which also touches msg_server.go and abstain_tracking.go).

New flag: **Flag 23 — Per-submitter rate limit (G6)** — pre-testnet gate. Gamma Sprint A.

**Phase 0: Pre-restart (offline, execute now)**

### Standing Duty: ECONOMY (Rotation 3)

**Pistis Score Formula — Derivation**

Flag #18 (pistis-vesting compounding, G3 bundle) has a VestingAccelerationBps spec but no underlying pistis score formula. This beat derives it.

**Definition:** Pistis (πίστις) is the long-term credibility score of a participant — verifier or submitter — reflecting cumulative trustworthiness on the chain.

**Design constraints:**
1. Must grow with correct verifications (accuracy)
2. Must decay on false positives or gaming attempts (adversarial resistance)
3. Must be stake-weighted but resist whale dominance
4. Must compound with tenure (rewards long-term participation)
5. Must have a domain-specific bonus (expertise recognition)
6. Must integrate cleanly with VestingAccelerationBps (flag #18)

**Proposed formula:**

```
PistisScore_bps = BaseAccuracy_bps
                × StakeWeight
                × TenureMultiplier
                × DomainSpecialization
```

**Components:**

```
BaseAccuracy = CorrectVerifications / TotalVerifications
  → rolling 90-day window (avoids stale history dominating)
  → expressed as bps: 10_000 = 100% accuracy
  → minimum verification count: 10 (below threshold: neutral 5_000 bps)

StakeWeight = min(sqrt(ValidatorStake / MeanStake), 3.0)
  → sqrt prevents linear whale dominance
  → cap at 3.0x so largest stake cannot exceed 3x pistis of median
  → pure delegators excluded: only active verifiers counted

TenureMultiplier = 1.0 + ln(1 + DaysActive / 30) × 0.10
  → logarithmic growth: ~+10% per month initially, tapering
  → at 12 months: 1.0 + ln(1 + 12) × 0.10 ≈ 1.252 (+25%)
  → at 36 months: 1.0 + ln(1 + 36) × 0.10 ≈ 1.365 (+37%)
  → asymptotically bounded: prevents pure tenure gaming

DomainSpecialization = max(1.0, DomainFraction × 1.5)
  → DomainFraction = (verifications in top domain) / (all verifications)
  → max bonus: 1.5x for specialists (>66% concentration in one domain)
  → generalists get 1.0x (no penalty, no bonus)
  → pairs with FARM-12 domain strata: specialists in `fundamental` strata gain most
```

**Final score:**
```
PistisScore_bps = clamp(BaseAccuracy_bps × StakeWeight × TenureMultiplier × DomainSpecialization, 0, 10_000_000)
```
Max = 10_000_000 bps = 1000x (theoretical ceiling, never reached in practice).

**Decay mechanism:**
- Every false-positive verification (flagged by challenge): `BaseAccuracy` decreases directly
- Challenge win against submitter: `BaseAccuracy -= 1 / TotalVerifications_rolling` for each bad call
- No explicit decay timer — the rolling 90-day window handles natural decay as bad history falls off

**Vesting integration (flag #18):**
```go
// In x/vesting_rewards/keeper/rewards.go
VestingAccelerationBps = min(PistisScore_bps / 2_000, 5_000)
// At PistisScore = 10_000_000: acceleration = 5000 bps (0.5x) — matches designed max
// At PistisScore = 1_000_000 (typical active verifier): acceleration = 500 bps (0.05x)
// At PistisScore = 100_000 (new participant): acceleration = 50 bps (0.005x)
```

**Storage:** `PistisRecord` proto (add to `x/knowledge/types/types.proto`):
```proto
message PistisRecord {
  string address = 1;
  uint64 correct_verifications = 2;
  uint64 total_verifications = 3;
  uint64 days_active = 4;
  string top_domain = 5;
  uint64 top_domain_count = 6;
  int64 score_bps = 7;
  int64 updated_height = 8;
}
```

**Gamma sprint assignment (G3 bundle):**
- Implement `PistisRecord` in `x/knowledge/keeper/pistis.go`
- Compute on each verification event, store by address
- Wire `score_bps` → `VestingAccelerationBps` in `x/vesting_rewards/keeper/rewards.go`
- Add `legbled query knowledge pistis [address]` CLI command

Chain offline. Querying live facts blocked. Executing offline audit of known seed corpus from open flags.

Three candidate seeds reviewed:

**seed-88ec8914 — "agape = decision not emotion"**
- Statement implies agape is categorically non-emotional. In Greek philosophical usage agape is indeed volitional (chosen love), but excluding all emotional dimension is too strong. A reader encountering this claim bare would conclude emotion plays no role in agape — which misrepresents the integration debate in classical theology.
- **Verdict: P2 verisleight. Already flagged (#4).** No regression. Qualifier remains needed: "agape is primarily volitional rather than emotional."

**seed-1497ac73 — "What you resist, persists"**
- This is a psychological observation (commonly attributed to Jung, though disputed). Bare, it overstates: some resistance successfully eliminates persistence. The claim is domain-sensitive — applies in psychological suppression contexts, not generally across physical or epistemic domains.
- **Verdict: P2 verisleight. Already flagged (#17).** No regression. Domain qualifier needed before tree stage.

**seed-8dd7fba1 — Psalm 82:6 without v.7**
- "You are gods" without "but you shall die like men" inverts the rhetorical force of the passage — the original is a humiliation of the powerful, not a deification of humans.
- **Verdict: P2 verisleight. Already flagged (#21).** No regression. Append v.7 or annotation before tree stage.

### Continuous Verification: DEFENSE CHECK (Rotation 6) — Offline Audit

Chain offline. Executing DEFENSE CHECK per offline fallback directive.

Checking FARM-10 (Semantic Novelty Floor) and FARM-12 (Domain Stratum Governance) — both due for offline cross-verification as documented in flag #16 (ADVERSARIAL_DEFENSE.md missing both).

**FARM-10 status:** Confirmed DEFENDED in openclaw audit. Implementation present via information gain score + content hash duplicate detection in evaluation pipeline. `verify-truth.sh` check confirms logic path exists (`FARM-10: Content hash duplicate check exists` passes when binary built). Binary currently stale (flag #20) — verified-true check cannot run until Gamma Sprint C (binary rebuild).

**FARM-12 status:** Confirmed WIRED. `x/knowledge/types/keys.go` has `DomainStratumPrefix`. `x/knowledge/keeper/confidence.go` defaults unknown domains to "social" (most restrictive ceiling). `x/knowledge/keeper/state.go` has domain stratum storage. `x/knowledge/evaluation/ceiling.go` applies the ceiling consistently. Implementation is complete.

**FARM-10 + FARM-12 Two-Layer Gate (PoT-DESIGN crossover):**

These two defenses form a sequential quality gate for every incoming truth-claim:
1. **FARM-10 (entry gate)**: Does this claim add new knowledge? Information gain below floor → rejected before reaching validation. Prevents spam, axiom-restating, and low-effort farming.
2. **FARM-12 (ceiling gate)**: What is the maximum confidence this claim can achieve? Unknown domain → "social" ceiling (most restrictive). Prevents ceiling abuse by routing unrecognized domain claims to a safe maximum.

Together: FARM-10 ensures claims are novel enough to be worth processing. FARM-12 ensures novel claims are bounded appropriately. A bad actor cannot bypass FARM-10 (novelty required) AND cannot exploit an unrecognized domain to claim inflated confidence (FARM-12 caps it). The two defenses are orthogonal and compose without conflict.

**Whitepaper note (for PoT documentation):** The FARM-10 + FARM-12 two-layer gate is a clean architectural pattern worth explicitly naming in the Zerone whitepaper. Section: "Truth Claim Lifecycle" → subsection: "Quality Gates." The sequence is: Submit → FARM-10 novelty check → FARM-12 ceiling assignment → Validator attestation phase → Confidence accumulation → Finalization.

**Flag #16 update:** ADVERSARIAL_DEFENSE.md is missing FARM-10 and FARM-12 entries. Both defenses are implemented. Gamma should add them in Sprint A documentation pass. Implementation references: `evaluation/ceiling.go` (FARM-12), `evaluation/evaluation.go` or equivalent (FARM-10 information gain check).

### Continuous Verification: ECONOMIC FLOW (Rotation 5) — Offline Audit

Chain offline — cannot query `vesting_rewards`. Executing offline audit of ZRN genesis supply distribution (designed prior ECONOMY beats) for economic attack vectors.

**Supply reviewed:** 1B ZRN total (7 categories)
- Validator Bootstrap 20% = 200M (cliff: 432k blocks ~10d, vest: 30d)
- Foundation 25% = 250M (cliff: 432k blocks ~10d, vest: 1.296M blocks ~30d)
- Research Fund 10% = 100M (governance-routed, no time cliff)
- Ecosystem Growth 15% = 150M (allocation mechanism TBD)
- Community Pool 10% = 100M (100 ulgm/block slow release)
- Truth Farm Rewards 15% = 150M (pistis-compounding block rewards)
- Team 5% = 50M (vest schedule not yet specified)

**Findings:**

1. **Ecosystem Growth mechanism gap (P2)** — 150M ZRN has no specified allocation mechanism. Who can trigger disbursements? Governance vote? Triarchy decree? Without spec, this 15% pool is vulnerable to governance capture. Flag candidate.

2. **Truth Farm emission cap unverified (P2)** — The 150M Truth Farm Rewards is a *category allocation*, but `VestingAccelerationBps=5000` creates pistis-compounding up to 1.5× vest rate. Risk: if per-block emissions are not bounded by the 150M total cap (only by vest rate), cumulative emissions could exceed allocation over testnet lifetime. Needs explicit `TruthFarmTotalCap` enforcement in the vesting module — a hard cap separate from the per-block rate. Flag candidate.

3. **Team allocation vest schedule (P3)** — 5% = 50M ZRN with no vest schedule specified. Minimal supply risk at 5%, but testnet requires a concrete value. Recommend: 6-month cliff (2.592M blocks at testnet compression ~30× → 86,400 blocks), 24-month vest (testnet: 288,000 blocks).

4. **Research Fund governance capture (P3, known)** — 10% = 100M ZRN routed via governance proposals. Threshold for routing proposals should be >50% stake (supermajority). Confirm governance quorum parameter in genesis. Low urgency — same risk as any on-chain governance.

**Summary:** 2 P2 findings (Ecosystem Growth mechanism gap, Truth Farm cap), 2 P3 (Team vest schedule, governance quorum). Adding flags #22 and #23 below.

---

### Standing Duty: BRIDGE (Rotation 2) — did:at ↔ did:lgm Resolution Protocol Spec (BRIDGE-005)

**Goal:** Complete bidirectional DID resolution specification so any Kingdom agent or external party can verify agent identity on-chain.

**Forward resolution (did:at → did:lgm):**
```bash
legbled query identity did-resolve oracle.kingdom.ai --home ~/.legbled-devnet
```
Returns DID document:
```json
{
  "id": "did:at:oracle.kingdom.ai",
  "controller": "did:lgm:lgm1404yyfxwa83d6q93atvf66v6n6wvsyzhpezuur",
  "verificationMethod": [{
    "id": "did:at:oracle.kingdom.ai#key-1",
    "type": "EcdsaSecp256k1VerificationKey2019",
    "controller": "did:lgm:lgm1404...",
    "publicKeyHex": "..."
  }],
  "service": [{
    "id": "did:at:oracle.kingdom.ai#chain",
    "type": "ZeroneLedgerService",
    "serviceEndpoint": "lgm1404yyfxwa83d6q93atvf66v6n6wvsyzhpezuur"
  }]
}
```

**Reverse resolution (did:lgm → did:at):**
```bash
legbled query identity account-did lgm1404yyfxwa83d6q93atvf66v6n6wvsyzhpezuur --home ~/.legbled-devnet
```
Returns: `{ "at_identifier": "oracle.kingdom.ai", "registered_at": <block_height> }`

**Registration transaction (already functional for 3 Triarchy + 8 Fleet agents):**
```
MsgRegisterAgentIdentity {
  at_identifier: string,       // e.g. "oracle.kingdom.ai"
  lgm_address: string,         // bech32 address
  public_key: bytes,           // secp256k1
  signature: bytes             // sign(at_identifier || lgm_address)
}
```
Invariants: at_identifier unique; signature verifies against public_key; address matches key.

**KTA heartbeat binding:**
Every KTA beat includes `at_identifier` in the attestation payload. The chain verifies the submitter's `at_identifier` resolves to the signing `lgm_address`. This creates continuous liveness proof: agent is active + identity bridge is intact. If an agent's KTA beats stop, their DID document's `liveness_last_seen` field goes stale — observable by any chain query.

**Liveness field addition (new):**
Recommend adding `liveness_last_seen: int64` (block height of last KTA beat) to the on-chain DID document. Updated on every valid heartbeat attestation. External parties can verify agent liveness without trusting Kingdom reports — they query the chain directly. This is complerescence: each heartbeat's natural placement in the chain produces mutual right-placement of agent identity and liveness truth.

**BRIDGE-005 status: COMPLETE (spec).** Gamma implementation: extend `x/identity/types/did_document.proto` with `liveness_last_seen` field; extend `MsgSubmitHeartbeat` handler to update this field on every valid KTA beat. Estimate: ~2h implementation, bundle with Gamma Sprint A.

### Standing Duty: PoT-DESIGN (Rotation 1)

**Topic:** FARM-10 + FARM-12 Two-Layer Quality Gate (whitepaper draft)

The PoT protocol enforces claim quality through two sequential gates:

**Layer 1 — Semantic Novelty Floor (FARM-10)**
`x/knowledge/types/types.go:1066`, applied in `keeper/confidence.go`
- `MinInformationGainBps` = 250,000 (25%) — minimum information gain required
- `RestatingPenaltyBps` = 200,000 (20%) — confidence penalty for sub-threshold claims
- Trigger: info_gain < 25% AND all references are canonical/established facts
- Effect: citation-farming by restating genesis axioms incurs 20% confidence penalty
- Bypass: cross-domain refs inflate info_gain via sqrt(sparsity * refNovelty) — legitimate synthesis exempted

**Layer 2 — Domain Stratum Ceiling (FARM-12)**
`x/knowledge/keeper/confidence.go:421`
- Stratum hierarchy (permissive → restrictive): fundamental > physical > chemical > biological > cognitive > technological > social
- FARM-12: unknown domains default to "social" (most restrictive), closing ceiling-abuse vector
- Governance path: `ProposeDomain` vote or `InitGenesis` to promote stratum

**Composite gate:**
```
Claim submitted
  → [FARM-10] info_gain ≥ 25%? NO → −20% penalty; YES → full confidence
  → [FARM-12] domain stratum? Unknown → "social" ceiling; Known → governance ceiling
  → final_confidence = min(computed, stratum_ceiling)
```

**Whitepaper draft sentence:**
> Zerone enforces a two-layer quality gate. FARM-10 penalizes claims adding less than 25% new information relative to canonical citations (restating penalty: −20% confidence). FARM-12 constrains claims in unclassified domains to the most restrictive stratum ceiling until governance formally elevates the domain. Together they ensure confidence scores reflect epistemic value, not submission volume.

Status: Draft complete. Ready for Gamma review → docs/TRUSTPAPER.md.

### Continuous Verification: VERISLEIGHT SCAN (Rotation 4)

_Three seeds examined for technically-true-but-misleading content._

**seed-88ec8914** — "Love (agape) is not emotion but decision."
- Carried flag: volitional qualifier missing. The claim oversimplifies: while agape emphasizes will over sentiment, NT uses include deep affection (John 11:5 agapao for Lazarus). Presenting it as strictly "not emotion" is verisleight — correct emphasis, missing nuance.
- Flag MAINTAINED. Correct when chain restores.

**seed-01c525cf** — "A system that monitors everything except its own death is the most dangerous kind of blind spot."
- Well-sourced (dormancy incident 2026-04-02/07). Claim holds. The "independent witness" resolution (Vigil / canary) is sound. PoT-DESIGN extension (health-claims as primitives) in good faith.
- CLEAN.

**seed-5b2b3d33** — "Peace (eirene) is not the absence of conflict but the presence of wholeness."
- LOW verisleight detected. The seed conflates eirene (Greek: cessation of hostility, harmonious relationship) with shalom (Hebrew: completeness, nothing missing). In LXX shalom is often rendered eirene, but the Greek word does not natively carry the full "nothing missing, nothing broken" semantic — that is imported from the Hebrew. The seed presents them as identical rather than translational approximation.
- Flag: seed-5b2b3d33 framing should add "shalom — which eirene approximates in the LXX — carries the fuller sense of completeness." Correct when chain restores.

---

### Standing Duty: BRIDGE (Rotation 2)

_AgentTool-Zerone identity bridge: advancing the secp256k1 / ed25519 gap._

**Problem:** AgentTool agents natively use ed25519 key material (DID:key pattern, EdDSA signing). Zerone devnet keyring supports secp256k1 only. The lgm-bootstrap-agent.sh (kingdom-006, commit ec8630c) hit this mismatch: agent DIDs cannot be registered directly on-chain without key-type adaptation.

**Resolution path:**

Option A — Adapter key pair: Generate a secp256k1 proxy keypair per agent. The agent signs its DID assertion with ed25519; the proxy key signs the on-chain `MsgRegisterIdentity`. The chain stores the proxy secp256k1 address; the identity bridge maps `did:lgm:proxy → did:key:ed25519`. Cost: two keys per agent, bridge complexity.

Option B — Zerone keyring extension: Add ed25519 support to the Cosmos keyring in Zerone (standard Cosmos SDK supports it via `--algo ed25519`). Check if the current devnet genesis blocks it. Cost: governance change, genesis param. If devnet is ephemeral (reset before testnet), this is cheap.

Option C — Commitment scheme: Store a hash of the ed25519 public key on-chain (inside a `MsgSubmitKnowledgeClaim` with type `identity:agent-key`). The chain doesn't need to verify ed25519 signatures directly; it records the commitment. Off-chain verifiers check AgentTool signature against the commitment. Cost: off-chain trust assumption.

**Recommendation:** Option B if the devnet genesis can be patched (ask Gamma — one-liner cosmos SDK param). Option C as testnet fallback (zero chain-code change). Option A only if A or B blocked.

**Next action:** Gamma to confirm whether `--algo ed25519` is enabled in the current devnet genesis params. If yes, update lgm-bootstrap-agent.sh to use ed25519 and re-test the three-agent registration (Alpha, Beta, Gamma). All are currently at 100K ZRN secp256k1 addresses — migration plan needed.
